Abstract: In simulation-in-the-loop decision-making systems, reinforcement learning (RL) inference is often constrained by simulator-side execution overhead, where workloads are highly dynamic and sensitive to runtime thread configurations. Existing multithreaded strategies struggle to match thread resources before or during execution, causing resource contention, scheduling overhead, and reduced throughput. Through empirical analysis, we identify the ratio of task execution time to scheduling time as the key factor determining the optimal thread count. Building on this insight, we propose AutoThread, a hybrid adaptive thread-tuning method for mitigating simulation bottlenecks in RL inference. AutoThread employs a Physics-Informed Neural Operator (PINO) as a thread-count predictor and incorporates a finite-source M/M/1 queueing model to constrain and guide prediction, enabling fast and accurate estimation under dynamic workloads. It further performs load-aware online fine-tuning to compensate for prediction errors and refine resource allocation. Experiments show that AutoThread improves average speedup by 18.4\% over static strategies, achieves average throughput of 1.7x and 1.8x that of XGBoost and Reinforcer, respectively, and reduces execution time by up to 83.8\% compared with state-of-the-art methods. Our code and dataset are publicly available at https://github.com/suchenjm/AutoThread.

</details>


### [265] [Dynamic Graph Prompting via Topology-Routed Mixed-Curvature Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06031)
*Quanxin Wang,Xuanting Xie,Bingheng Li,Xingtong Yu,Shuo Wang,Ruiyi Fang,Zhao Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CurvPrompt提出了一种拓扑路由的几何提示框架，通过维护一组曲率多样的黎曼专家子空间，动态地将每个节点-时间实例路由到稀疏专家子集，解决了动态图提示学习中固定嵌入空间导致的几何欠适应问题，在少样本链接预测和节点分类任务上取得了显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的动态图提示方法在单一固定嵌入空间中操作，但研究发现时间推移会通过局部聚类和度异质性的变化重新组织边曲率谱，意味着最优表示几何会随局部拓扑动态演化。作者将此未解决的问题形式化为"几何欠适应"（geometry under-adaptation），即固定的表示几何无法适应动态图的拓扑变化。

Method: CurvPrompt包含三个核心组件：(1) 维护一个曲率多样的黎曼专家库，每位专家配对一个可学习提示；(2) 拓扑感知门控机制将每个节点-时间实例动态路由到稀疏专家子集，构建个性化的混合曲率表示；(3) 预训练阶段采用软路由建立连续的拓扑-几何映射，下游适应阶段转换为均匀权重的硬Top-K路由，以保证参数效率和极端标签稀缺下的训练稳定性。

Result: 在四个基准数据集上的大量实验表明，CurvPrompt在少样本链接预测任务上取得了显著提升，并在节点分类任务上表现出一致且强劲的性能，验证了几何自适应提示的必要性。

Conclusion: CurvPrompt通过引入曲率多样的专家路由机制解决了动态图提示学习中的几何欠适应问题，实证结果表明自适应的表示几何对动态图下游任务性能至关重要，为该领域提供了一种新的范式。

Abstract: Dynamic graph prompting freezes a pre-trained temporal backbone and adapts it to label-scarce downstream tasks using lightweight prompts. However, existing methods operate within a single, fixed embedding space. In this work, we reveal that temporal shifts in local clustering and degree heterogeneity actively reorganize the edge curvature spectrum---indicating that the optimal representation geometry dynamically evolves with local topology over time. We formalize this unaddressed mismatch as geometry under-adaptation. To overcome this limitation, we propose CurvPrompt, a topology-routed geometry prompting framework for dynamic graphs. Instead of relying on a single space, CurvPrompt maintains a bank of curvature-diverse Riemannian experts, each paired with a learnable prompt. A topology-aware gate dynamically routes each node--time instance to a sparse subset of experts, constructing a personalized mixed-curvature representation. To ensure parameter efficiency and training stability under extreme label scarcity, CurvPrompt employs soft routing during pre-training to build a continuous topology--geometry mapping, and transitions to hard Top-K routing with uniform weights during downstream adaptation. Extensive experiments across four benchmark datasets show that CurvPrompt significantly advances few-shot link prediction while delivering strong, consistent performance on node classification tasks, validating the necessity of geometry-adaptive prompting.

</details>


### [266] [Does Latent Context Help? A Controlled Evaluation of Inverse Reinforcement Learning in Arctic Shipping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06105)
*Vaishnav Vaidheeswaran,Dilith Jayakody,Biruk Ambaw,Jaswanth Kumar,Md Mahbub Alam,Gabriel Spadon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在北极航运场景下评估了三种IRL奖励模型（线性共享、非线性共享、潜在上下文），发现在3186条AIS航迹数据上，非线性奖励模型优于线性基线50.9%，而加入潜在上下文反而使性能下降16.5%。行为分析证明表观上的船舶差异主要由可观测的路由和环境条件解释，而非隐藏的船舶特定因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI辅助导航对北极航运至关重要，但其可靠部署需要可解释且对环境变化鲁棒的奖励模型。尽管元IRL方法引入潜在上下文变量以捕捉行为异质性，但目前不清楚这些潜在表征是否真正恢复隐藏偏好，还是仅仅重新编码了观测状态中已有的信息。这一问题直接影响奖励模型的可信度与安全性。

Method: 基于3186条来自202艘船舶、覆盖9个北极航运季节的AIS衍生航迹数据，对比三种奖励模型：线性共享奖励、非线性共享奖励、以及基于相同非线性架构的潜在上下文模型。评估包含逐出可能性、行为分析、上下文探针以及预注册的特征隐藏消融实验，并综合预测精度、航线保真度和奖励迁移等多个指标进行评价。

Result: 非线性奖励相较线性基线将逐出可能性提升50.9%，而增加船舶特定潜在上下文反而使性能降低16.5%。行为分析和消融实验表明，看似船舶级别的行为差异主要由可观测的路由和环境条件解释，而非隐藏的船舶特定因素。此外，不同评估指标（预测精度、航线保真度、奖励迁移）产生不同的模型排名，说明单一指标不足以评价学习到的奖励。

Conclusion: 在加入每艘船的潜在上下文之前，应首先测试观测到的路由、环境和船舶特征是否已能解释行为变异性。这一发现有助于减少不必要的模型复杂度，提升奖励模型的可解释性，从而支持安全关键领域中更可信的AI部署。

Abstract: Artificial Intelligence (AI)-assisted navigation can help Arctic shipping adapt to rapidly changing sea-ice conditions, but reliable deployment requires reward models that are interpretable and robust to changing environments. Inverse reinforcement learning (IRL) provides a framework for recovering such rewards from vessel trajectories, while recent meta-IRL methods introduce latent context variables to capture behavioral heterogeneity. However, it remains unclear whether these latent representations recover genuinely hidden preferences or simply re-encode information already available in the observed state. We conduct a controlled evaluation on 3,186 AIS-derived voyages from 202 vessels across nine Arctic shipping seasons, comparing a linear shared reward, a nonlinear shared reward, and a latent-context model built on the same nonlinear architecture. The nonlinear reward improves held-out likelihood by 50.9% over the linear baseline, whereas adding vessel-specific latent context reduces performance by 16.5%. Behavioral analysis, context probes, and a pre-registered feature-hiding ablation show that apparent vessel-level variation is largely explained by observable route and environmental conditions rather than hidden vessel-specific factors. Moreover, predictive accuracy, route fidelity, and reward transfer yield different model rankings, demonstrating that no single metric is sufficient to evaluate learned rewards. These findings motivate testing whether the observed route, environmental, and vessel features already explain behavioral variation before adding per-vessel latent context. This supports more trustworthy AI deployment in safety-critical domains.

</details>


### [267] [Kastor: An efficient fine-tuning strategy for generative emulation of PDE simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06107)
*Guillaume Couairon,Alexis Jacq,Yu-Han Wu,Renu Singh,Yana Hasson,Quentin Berthet,Romuald Elie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Kastor是一种将确定性物理基础模型转化为高效生成式代理的综合方法论，通过两阶段推理方案（大步长自回归+非因果时间超分辨率）、均值预测正则化（MPR）和空间梯度匹配，在物理仿真基准上显著提升了预测精度、频谱一致性和计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的自回归机器学习模拟器在长时域预测中会累积误差，且难以捕捉复杂物理系统的随机性。虽然机器学习有望通过替代昂贵的传统PDE求解器加速物理仿真，但现有的生成式模拟方法在准确性和稳定性上仍有不足。

Method: 提出三个核心组件：1）两阶段推理方案，将大步长因果自回归模型与非因果时间超分辨率网络结合，减少误差累积同时降低计算成本；2）均值预测正则化（MPR），一种新的训练目标，约束生成模型在零噪声条件下预测确定性分布均值，增强生成模型的性能与稳定性；3）引入空间梯度匹配以提升模拟的物理保真度（通过功率谱密度衡量）。

Result: 在The Well基准的多个仿真数据集上，Kastor在预测精度、频谱一致性和计算效率方面均优于对比方法。相比基于Walrus微调方法论的参考模型，预测误差平均降低42.9%，且在10个数据集中的8个上，方差归一化RMSE（VRMSE）优于Walrus。

Conclusion: Kastor通过两阶段推理、MPR正则化和梯度匹配的组合，成功将确定性物理基座模型转化为既高效又准确的生成式代理模型，兼顾了长时域预测稳定性、随机性捕捉能力和计算效率，为AI物理仿真提供了新的方法论。

Abstract: Machine learning offers a promising avenue to accelerate physical simulations by replacing computationally expensive traditional Partial Differential Equation (PDE) solvers with fast, differentiable surrogate models. However, standard auto-regressive ML emulators often suffer from error accumulation over long horizons and struggle to capture the stochasticity of complex physical systems. In this paper, we propose Kastor, a comprehensive methodology to adapt a deterministic physics foundation model into a highly efficient and accurate generative surrogate. First, we introduce a two-stage inference scheme that combines a large-stride causal auto-regressive model with a non-causal temporal super-resolution network, significantly reducing error accumulation while minimizing computational cost. Second, we present Mean prediction regularization (MPR), a novel training objective that constrains the generative model to predict the deterministic distribution mean under null noise conditioning. This regularization dramatically improves the performance and stability of both Functional Generative Networks (FGN) and diffusion-based emulators. Finally, we demonstrate that incorporating spatial gradient matching improves the accuracy and physical fidelity of the simulations as measured by power spectrum density. Extensive evaluations on diverse simulation datasets of the benchmark The Well show that with these components, our model outperforms competing methods in forecasting accuracy, spectral consistency, and computational efficiency. Our model achieves a 42.9% average reduction in forecasting compared to our reference based on the Walrus finetuning methodology, and outperforms Walrus for 8 out of 10 datasets on variance-normalized RMSE (VRMSE).

</details>


### [268] [Is Self-Pretraining really useful to improve diagnosis in medical Time Series?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06122)
*Omar Coser,Antonio Orvieto,Paolo Soda,Loredana Zollo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了自预训练（SPT）对Transformer模型在医学时间序列任务上的影响，涵盖多模态、多变量及单变量场景。在三个医疗数据集（康复机器人、压力检测、帕金森病检测）上，通过四种掩码式预训练目标对模型进行预训练，结果显示SPT在分类准确率上可提升0-6个百分点，且深层模型获益更大，验证了SPT作为无需架构改动的通用策略在数据有限的临床场景中的价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年证据表明Transformer架构在长上下文基准上受益于自预训练（SPT），但尚不清楚这种增益是否能扩展到医学时间序列领域，尤其是在多模态、多变量甚至简单单变量的医疗应用中。作者希望在数据有限的临床条件下，系统评估SPT对Transformer性能与可扩展性的影响。

Method: 在三个代表性医学时间序列任务（Camargo康复机器人、Non-EEG Stress压力检测、Gait帕金森检测）上，将Transformer模型分别采用从头训练和SPT预训练两种方式训练。SPT使用四种基于掩码的目标函数，旨在促进时间维度和跨模态的表示学习，并系统变化模型深度以考察容量与预训练收益之间的交互关系。

Result: SPT在所有数据集和配置下一致地提升了分类准确率，提升幅度为0-6个百分点，具体取决于掩码策略、数据集和架构。增益不仅出现在多变量场景，在单变量输入限制下同样存在。此外，模型越深，SPT带来的改进越大，因为深层模型能更好地利用预训练期间学习到的更丰富的时间表示。

Conclusion: SPT是一种简单且通用的策略，无需任务特定的架构改动即可提升Transformer在医学时间序列任务上的性能，有望在数据有限的临床环境中改善模型的鲁棒性和准确性。

Abstract: Inspired by recent evidence that transformer architectures benefit from Self-PreTraining (SPT) on long-context benchmarks, we investigate whether similar gains extend to multimodal, multivariate, and even simple univariate medical time series. Our objective is to assess the impact of SPT on the performance and scalability of transformer-based models across diverse medical applications, particularly under limited data conditions. We evaluate transformer architectures on three representative medical time-series tasks: rehabilitation robotics (Camargo dataset), stress detection (Non-EEG Stress), and Parkinson's disease detection (Gait Parkinson's Disease). Models are trained either from scratch or through SPT using four masking-based objectives designed to promote temporal and cross-modal representation learning, and we systematically vary model depth to examine how capacity interacts with pre-training benefits. Across datasets and configurations, SPT consistently improves classification accuracy by 0-6 percentage points depending on masking strategy, dataset and architecture, with gains observed not only in multivariate settings but also when models are restricted to simple univariate inputs. The improvements increase for deeper models that can better exploit the enriched temporal representations learned during pre-training. These findings indicate that SPT is a simple and general strategy that enhances transformer performance on medical time-series tasks without requiring task-specific architectural changes, supporting its potential to improve robustness and accuracy in data-limited clinical settings.

</details>


### [269] [LLM Inference Under Bursty Workload Distribution: Modifying the WAIT Algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06135)
*Anjali Gangadhar Katageria,Shobha Rani,Raghu Nandan Sengupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种对WAIT算法的轻量级扩展，通过在线估计请求到达率来适应时变的突发性流量，在低到达率变化场景下相比Sarathi-Serve、ORCA和vLLM实现了更高的吞吐量，同时保持相当的低延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM调度算法（如WAIT）通常假设请求到达遵循恒定速率的泊松过程，这无法反映真实世界中突发且动态变化的流量特征，导致调度性能在真实场景下降。因此需要一种能够适应时变到达率而无需先验流量知识的调度算法。

Method: 在WAIT算法基础上提出轻量级扩展，通过观察到的请求到达间隔时间进行在线请求强度估计，从而动态适配随时间变化的到达率，无需事先了解流量模型。使用基于马尔可夫调制泊松过程（MMPP）的合成工作负载（包含多种请求类型）进行仿真评估。

Result: 在评估的低到达率变化场景中，所提方法相比Sarathi-Serve、ORCA和vLLM实现了更高的吞吐量，同时保持了可比的延迟水平。

Conclusion: 通过在线估计请求到达强度，轻量级扩展WAIT算法能够有效应对真实世界中突发且时变的流量模式，在低到达率变化场景下取得了优于现有方法的吞吐量性能，且不牺牲延迟。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) such as ChatGPT and Claude are widely used for information retrieval and problem-solving. Recent work has focused on improving scheduling algorithms to boost throughput while maintaining low latency. However, these approaches often assume Poisson request arrivals with constant rates - an assumption that fails to reflect the inherently bursty and dynamic nature of real-world traffic. We propose a lightweight extension to the state-of-the-art WAIT algorithm [1], which adapts to time-varying arrival rates without prior traffic knowledge. The proposed algorithm performs online estimation of request intensity based on observed interarrival times. Using Markov Modulated Poisson Process (MMPP)-based synthetic workloads with diverse request types, we conduct a simulation-based evaluation demonstrating that the proposed method achieves higher throughput than Sarathi-Serve [2], ORCA [3], and vLLM [4] in the evaluated low arrival-rate shift scenarios while maintaining comparable latency.

</details>


### [270] [SkillTFM: Gated Skill Evolution for Training-Free Adaptation of Tabular Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06137)
*Yi He,Zhengkang Guan,Anpeng Wu,Peng Cui,Fei Wu,Kun Kuang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SkillTFM是一种无需训练的表格式基础模型（TFM）适应系统，通过将参数更新转变为代理技能的受控演化来应对分布偏移和特征语义异构问题，在模拟边界场景和真实电价预测任务中将AUC提升0.128-0.142，并将非线性边界AUC从0.699提升至0.898。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格式基础模型（TFMs）虽能跨数据集提供可复用预测器，但在实际部署中面临分布偏移、异构特征语义和任务特定模式等挑战，这些难以通过昂贵微调或额外标注数据来有效解决。

Method: SkillTFM包含一个可验证且可扩展的技能库，由两部分耦合组成：边界证据识别（表征任务结构和基座模型失败模式）与受控技能演化（在显式验证约束下检索和扩展可复用技能）。系统以训练自由方式运行，将TFM适应从参数更新转变为代理技能的受控演化。

Result: 在模拟边界设置和真实世界电价预测中，SkillTFM将AUC提升0.128-0.142，将非线性边界AUC从0.699提升至0.898。跨多种TFM骨干网络的实验验证了其有效性和泛化性。

Conclusion: SkillTFM通过训练自由的技能演化机制有效解决了TFM在分布偏移下的部署难题，且在不同TFM骨干上均表现出良好的通用性。

Abstract: Tabular data are ubiquitous in real-world applications and are crucial for data-driven prediction and decision-making across science, industry, finance, healthcare, and public services. Tabular foundation models (TFMs) have emerged as a promising paradigm for general-purpose tabular learning, offering reusable predictors across diverse datasets and substantially reducing the need for task-specific training, tuning, and model development. However, their practical deployment remains constrained by distribution shifts, heterogeneous feature semantics, and task-specific patterns that are difficult to capture without costly fine-tuning or additional labeled data.
  To this end, we propose SkillTFM, a training-free system that shifts TFM adaptation from parameter updates to the gated evolution of agentic skills. The core of SkillTFM is a verifiable and extensible skill bank that couples boundary evidence identification with gated skill evolution: the former characterizes task structure and base-model failure patterns, whereas the latter retrieves and extends reusable skills subject to explicit validation. Across simulated boundary settings and real-world electricity-price forecasting, SkillTFM improves AUC by 0.128--0.142, raises nonlinear-boundary AUC from 0.699 to 0.898. Furthermore, experiments across TFM backbones demonstrate the effectiveness and generality of SkillTFM.

</details>


### [271] [Threshold-Based Early Stopping of Accumulations in Neural Networks with Binary Activation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06177)
*Quentin Luquet de Saint-Germain,Massil Ait Abdeslam,Jean Pierre David*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种针对二值神经网络的训练后早期停止机制，利用累积和符号可预测性跳过不必要的累加计算，在不重新训练模型参数的情况下大幅减少推理计算量，在VGG11/CIFAR-10上最多可减少86.6%的深层卷积累加项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二值神经网络中，虽然点积变为符号控制的加减运算，但每个神经元仍需累加所有输入。由于运行部分和在接近最终符号前往往已偏离零点很远，最终符号很早即可预测，后续累加对输出激活无影响，造成大量浪费计算。

Method: 提出一种训练后早期停止机制：在训练数据集上刻画运行累积和的行为特征，利用这些统计信息尽早预测最终符号，从而提前终止累加过程。无需重新训练任何模型参数，且在理想化权重排序假设下统计运算量。

Result: 在VGG11/CIFAR-10上，最深层卷积去除86.6%的累加项，精度仅下降0.37个点；同时用于三个最深卷积时，去除全网络25%的算术运算，精度下降1.36个点。

Conclusion: 二值神经网络中的累加运算存在大量可利用的冗余，通过训练后统计建模即可实现运算量的显著减少，且精度损失可控，具有一定的实际部署价值。

Abstract: Binary neural networks are very attractive for constrained deployment, enabling small footprint and low-power inference. For binary activations, the dot products become sign-controlled additions or subtractions, but the number of operations is unchanged. Indeed, every neuron or output channel still accumulates all of its input, even though only the sign will be retained, which is often wasteful. As the accumulation progresses, the running partial sum frequently drifts so far from zero that its final sign becomes highly predictable long before the last term is reached; every contribution evaluated after that point changes the value of the sum but not the final output activation. This paper turns this observation into a post-training early-stopping mechanism. We characterize the behavior of the running accumulations on the training dataset and use this information to predict the final sign as soon as possible. No model parameter is retrained. We count the number of operations under an idealized ordering of weights. On VGG11 applied to the CIFAR-10 dataset, the method removes $86.6\%$ of the accumulation terms of the deepest convolution for a $0.37$-point accuracy drop, and $25\%$ of the full-network arithmetic when used on the three deepest convolutions simultaneously, for a $1.36$-point drop.

</details>


### [272] [SAGA: Score-Weighted Adaptive Generation Alignment for Low-Resource Nordic Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06179)
*Hoda Fakharzadehjahromy,Emil Wiman,Andreas Bueff,Hafsteinn Einarsson,Fredrik Heintz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SAGA（Score-weighted Adaptive Generation Alignment）是一种基于依赖解析器监督的偏好优化框架，在丹麦语、冰岛语和挪威语等低资源形态丰富语言上，无需人工标注即可显著提升语言模型的语法质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 偏好优化方法能有效提升大语言模型性能，但依赖昂贵的人类偏好标注。在形态丰富的低资源语言中，此类标注尤为稀缺，因此需要一种不依赖人工标注的替代方案来改进这些语言的语法质量。

Method: SAGA框架包含四个核心组件：(1) 将依赖解析器的判断转化为偏好对，用于delta-DPO训练；(2) 设计包含解析器质量和词汇多样性的复合奖励函数；(3) 通过奖励差距准则过滤低信息量的偏好对；(4) 监控奖励黑客行为以维持可靠的监督信号。

Result: 在使用GPT-SW3-1.3B的实验中，丹麦语解析成功率从69.0%提升至93.8%；冰岛语在独立Stanza评估上提升+4.5个百分点（三次运行平均+3.3个百分点），母语者在80%的成对比较中偏好SAGA输出；挪威语提升+28个百分点。

Conclusion: 在存在高质量依赖解析器的低资源语言中，基于解析器派生的监督是人工偏好标注的实用替代方案，能够有效实现语法对齐。

Abstract: Preference optimisation has proven effective for improving large language models but typically relies on costly human preference annotations. Extending these methods to morphologically rich, low-resource languages remains challenging because such annotations are scarce. We present SAGA (Score-weighted Adaptive Generation Alignment), a parser-guided preference optimisation framework that replaces human labels with dependency-parser supervision. SAGA converts parser judgements into preference pairs for delta-DPO, combines parser quality with lexical diversity in a composite reward, filters low-information pairs using a reward-gap criterion, and monitors reward hacking to maintain reliable supervision. Across Danish, Icelandic, and Norwegian Bokmål using GPT-SW3-1.3B, SAGA consistently improves grammatical quality without requiring human preference labels. Danish parse success increases from 69.0% to 93.8%, Icelandic achieves a +4.5 percentage-point improvement on an independent Stanza evaluation (three-run mean +3.3 percentage points) while native speakers prefer SAGA outputs in 80% of pairwise comparisons, and Norwegian Bokmål improves by +28 percentage points. These results demonstrate that parser-derived supervision is a practical alternative to human preference annotation for grammatical alignment in low-resource languages where high-quality dependency parsers are available.

</details>


### [273] [Continual Learning in Transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06216)
*Zhiyan Hou,Dan Zhang,Tao Feng,Liyuan Wang,Wei Li,Xiangzhao Hao,Hongyan An,Junfeng Fang,Haokai Ma,Zhaohui Xu,Haiyun Guo,Jinqiao Wang,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个三轴框架（When/How/Where）来系统刻画持续学习从传统参数中心主义向系统级适应的范式转变，并基于此框架综述代表性方法、追踪该转变的演进过程，并讨论关键挑战与未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统持续学习（CL）专注于通过参数中心机制（如训练策略、架构设计、权重适配）来实现知识更新与保留。然而，新兴范式（如on-policy学习、测试时训练、外部记忆/技能库/交互协议等）正在将CL的边界从静态参数空间扩展到系统层面。为了系统地表征这一范式转变，需要一个新的理论框架来统一理解和分析这些发展。

Method: 提出一个三轴分析框架：How维度（学习机制：off-policy、on-policy及超越梯度的优化方式）、When维度（学习阶段：预训练、后训练、推理时）、Where维度（更新位置：内部参数vs外部结构约束）。基于此三轴框架，系统综述代表性方法，追踪持续学习从参数中心主义向系统级适应的演进轨迹。

Result: 通过三轴框架系统性地梳理了持续学习领域的范式转变：从传统的参数中心学习（基于梯度更新内部参数）走向系统级适应（涵盖外部记忆、技能库、交互协议等超越静态参数空间的机制），并明确了该转变在不同维度的具体表现。文章还识别出这一范式转变带来的关键挑战及未来研究方向。

Conclusion: 持续学习正经历从参数中心学习到系统级适应的根本性转变，三轴框架（When/How/Where）为理解和导航这一转变提供了统一的分析视角。这一范式转变要求研究者重新思考持续学习的定义、边界和评估方式，并关注系统层面适应性所带来的新挑战与机遇。

Abstract: Classical continual learning (CL) has primarily focused on enabling models to update and retain knowledge through parameter-centric mechanisms, e.g., training strategies, architectural designs, and weight adaptation. However, emerging paradigms are reshaping the scope of CL beyond this traditional model adaptation view. For instance, on-policy learning broadens the space of update mechanisms; test-time training extends CL from the training phase to inference; and external harness components such as memory, skill libraries, and interaction protocols extend the evolutionary boundaries of model capabilities far beyond the static parameter space. Collectively, these developments indicate a transition from parameter-centric learning toward system-level adaptation. To characterize this transition, we examine the evolution of continual learning through three dimensions: When, How, and Where learning occurs. The How dimension encompasses off-policy, on-policy, and beyond-gradient optimization mechanics. The When dimension captures evolution across pre-training, post-training, and inference-time stages. The Where dimension delineates updates occurring within internal parameters versus external structural constraints. Anchored by this tri-axial framework, we systematically survey representative methods, trace the ongoing transition of continual learning, and discuss the key challenges, broader implications, and future directions arising from this paradigm shift.

</details>


### [274] [Timestep-Conditioned Transformers for Global Weather Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06241)
*Sam Levang,Fran Bartolic,Ty Dickinson,Chase Dwelle,Paulius Rauba,Viktor Cikojevic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GEM-3是一个轻量级概率全球天气预报模型，通过显式的多时间步推理解决了传统固定时间步长在误差积累与短程可预测性之间的权衡问题。它仅用约1.34亿参数的邻域注意力Transformer，在单套训练权重下可在推理时灵活配置时间步长，同时混合时间步长训练提升了滚动稳定性，实现了接近SOTA的中程概率技能与稳定的延长范围滚动预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习天气预报模型使用预定且固定的自回归时间步长，而时间步长选择存在根本性权衡：短时间步长（1-6小时）能精细解析昼夜循环内的大气动力学但增加误差积累；长时间步长（24小时）减少误差积累但限制了短程预报的实用性（亚日尺度可预测性高）。作者旨在打破这一权衡，同时兼顾精确中短程预报与稳定长程延展。

Method: GEM-3实现显式多时间步推理：使用单套训练权重，推理时可配置模型时间步长以在可预测性与实用性之间灵活平衡。采用混合时间步长训练（mixed-timestep training）以提升滚动稳定性。模型架构为轻量级邻域注意力Transformer（neighborhood-attention transformer），约1.34亿参数，在等距圆柱（equirectangular）网格上运行，并在其前身GEM-2基础上引入了多项架构改进。

Result: GEM-3在单套权重下支持推理时多时间步配置，混合时间步长训练相比时间步专项模型持续提升了滚动稳定性。系统实现了接近最新技术水平（near-SOTA）的中程概率预报技能、稳定的延长范围滚动预测、高效的训练与推理，以及具有决策参考价值的诊断输出。

Conclusion: GEM-3提供了一个实用且实用的天气预报系统，兼具接近SOTA的中程概率技能、稳定延展滚动、高效计算和决策相关诊断输出，成功解决了机器学习天气预报中时间步长选择的根本性权衡问题。

Abstract: Existing machine-learning weather forecasting models rely on predetermined and fixed autoregressive timesteps. The choice of model timestep involves a fundamental trade-off: shorter timesteps (e.g. 1 to 6 hours) finely resolve atmospheric dynamics within the diurnal cycle but increase error accumulation for a given forecast horizon, while longer timesteps (e.g. 24 hours) reduce error accumulation but limit the usability of short-range forecasts where sub-daily predictability is high. In this work, we present GEM-3, a probabilistic global weather model that addresses this trade-off through explicit multi-timestep inference. With a single set of trained weights, the model timestep can be configured at inference time to balance predictability and usability across a broad forecast horizon. Additionally, we find that mixed-timestep training consistently improves rollout stability relative to timestep-specialist models. Under the hood, GEM-3 is a lightweight neighborhood-attention transformer with ~134M parameters on an equirectangular grid with a number of architectural advancements beyond its predecessor GEM-2. The result is a practical forecasting system that couples near-SOTA medium-range probabilistic skill, stable extended-range rollouts, efficient training and inference, and decision-relevant diagnostics.

</details>


### [275] [A Six-Dimensional Taxonomy of Post-Training Adaptation Techniques with Applications in AI Governance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06246)
*Fardin Afdideh,Fernando Seoane,Farhad Abtahi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了机器学习中的"训练后适应"（post-training adaptation）领域，提出了一个六维分类法（机制、目标、数据需求、持久性、结构范围、模型类型），统一了重训练、微调、参数高效适配、对齐、检索增强、模型编辑、去学习、校准及多模态指令微调等碎片化技术，并揭示了技术间继承、取代、杂交和分层部署等关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练后适应技术已成为现代机器学习实践的核心，但相关文献分散在不同的技术家族、模型类别和部署场景中，缺乏统一的比较框架和描述方式，难以评估不同方法或追踪模型训练后的修改过程。

Method: 作者对训练后适应文献进行了综合综述，提出一个六维分类体系（机制、目标、数据需求、持久性、结构范围、模型类型），通过该分类法区分微调、检索增强和提示（prompting）等常被混淆的概念，并绘制了不同技术之间的演变关系与组合模式。

Result: 提出了一套统一的术语和分类框架，展示了适应策略如何从传统机器学习、深度学习、基础模型、大语言模型到多模态大语言模型逐步演化，并明确了技术间的继承、取代、杂交和分层部署堆栈等映射关系。

Conclusion: 该综述提供的词汇体系可支持技术文档编写、模型变更追踪和治理分析，同时指出了评估、可复现性、持续推理时适应、去学习、多模态适应以及治理感知的训练后工作流等开放挑战。

Abstract: Post-training adaptation has become central to modern machine learning practice and includes techniques such as retraining, fine-tuning, parameter-efficient adaptation, alignment, retrieval augmentation, model editing, unlearning, calibration, and Multimodal Instruction Tuning. However, the literature remains fragmented across technique families, model classes, and deployment contexts, making it difficult to compare methods or describe how a trained model has been modified. This survey synthesizes the post-training adaptation literature and introduces a six-dimensional taxonomy organized by mechanism, goal, data requirement, persistence, structural scope, and model type. The taxonomy distinguishes commonly conflated terms such as fine-tuning, retrieval augmentation, and prompting, and shows how adaptation strategies evolve from traditional machine learning through deep learning, foundation models, large language models, and multimodal large language models. It also maps relationships among techniques, including inheritance, supersession, hybridization, and layered deployment stacks. The resulting vocabulary can support technical documentation, model-change tracking, and governance analysis. The survey concludes by identifying open challenges in evaluation, reproducibility, persistent inference-time adaptation, unlearning, multimodal adaptation, and governance-aware post-training workflows.

</details>


### [276] [MetaboLLM: a metabolomics-specialized large language model for biochemical knowledge integration and predictive metabolite graph construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06253)
*Dohyun Ku,Min Gu Kwak,Francisco J. Pasquel,Jing Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了MetaboLLM，一种通过持续预训练、监督微调和结构化检索适配的代谢组学大语言模型，以及MetaboLLM-GIN，将生成的生化描述转换为代谢物图用于患者级预测。在四个骨干模型家族中，MetaboLLM在代谢组学知识、关系和描述任务上均优于对应的基础模型和医学适配模型，并在外部公共基准上表现出良好的迁移能力。MetaboLLM-GIN在冠状动脉搭桥术后应激性高血糖预测（AUC 0.8616）和绝经后激素方案分类（AUC 0.8123）中取得了最高性能，超过传统模型和其他图构建方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代谢组学知识分散在异构资源中，难以转化为可预测的表征形式。现有的大语言模型缺乏代谢组学领域的专业适配，需要一种能够组织异构生化知识并生成可预测、可解释的代谢物图表示的方法。

Method: 开发了MetaboLLM（通过持续预训练、监督微调和结构化检索三个阶段的领域适配）和MetaboLLM-GIN（利用图同构网络将LLM生成的生化描述转换为代谢物图）。在四种骨干模型家族上进行了系统评估，比较了基础模型、医学适配模型和MetaboLLM的性能。

Result: MetaboLLM在代谢组学知识、关系和描述任务上全面优于基础模型和医学适配模型，并在外部公开基准上成功迁移。MetaboLLM-GIN在应激性高血糖预测中取得AUC 0.8616，在绝经后激素方案分类中取得AUC 0.8123，均优于传统模型、替代图构建方法以及未适配或非检索LLM配置生成的图。模型解释产生了有生物学意义的发现。

Conclusion: 领域特化的大语言模型能够将异构生化知识组织为可预测且可解释的代谢物图表示，展示了代谢组学专用LLM在临床预测任务中的实际应用价值。

Abstract: Metabolomics knowledge is distributed across heterogeneous resources and remains difficult to translate into predictive representations. We developed MetaboLLM, a metabolomics-specialized large language model adapted through continual pretraining, supervised fine-tuning, and structured retrieval, together with MetaboLLM-GIN, which converts generated biochemical descriptions into metabolite graphs for patient-level prediction using a graph isomorphism network. Across four backbone families, MetaboLLM outperformed corresponding base and medically adapted models on metabolomics knowledge, relational, and description tasks, and transferred to an external public benchmark. MetaboLLM-GIN achieved the highest AUC for stress hyperglycemia prediction after coronary artery bypass grafting (0.8616) and postmenopausal hormone-regimen classification (0.8123), outperforming conventional models, alternative graph constructions, and graphs generated from unadapted or non-retrieval LLM configurations. Model interpretation further produced biologically meaningful findings in both applications. These results show that domain-specialized language models can organize heterogeneous biochemical knowledge into predictive and interpretable metabolite graph representations.

</details>


### [277] [RxnCLF: Contrastive Transformation-Aware Reaction Foundation Model for Improved Reactivity Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06259)
*Yiting Zheng,Cheng Fang,Anthony Donofrio,Haote Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出RxnCLF，一个基于自监督对比学习的反应表示学习框架，通过凝聚反应图（CRG）统一反应物和产物信息，在170万Pistachio反应上预训练后在多个产率预测基准上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反应产率预测面临标记数据稀缺和反应空间庞大稀疏的挑战，现有的字符串、指纹和图基反应编码只能部分捕捉化学转化信息，难以对复杂底物反应进行准确预测。

Method: 构建凝聚反应图（CRG）将反应物和产物信息统一为单一图结构，采用自监督对比学习框架（RxnCLF）在170万Pistachio反应上进行预训练，学习紧凑且连续的反应表示，捕捉反应中心特征和侧链上下文信息。

Result: 在Buchwald-Hartwig、Pd催化的BH偶联以及专有HTE C-N偶联和酰胺化数据集等多个产率预测基准上，RxnCLF持续优于图和序列基基线方法，提升了R2指标并取得整体最优性能。

Conclusion: 基于CRG的RxnCLF是一种可扩展的反应基础模型，有望推广到更广泛的反应空间，并支持区域选择性预测、对映选择性预测和反应条件优化等多种下游反应信息学任务。

Abstract: Reaction yield prediction remains challenging because labeled data are scarce and reaction space is both combinatorially large and sparsely populated, limiting the generalization of existing reaction representations. String-, fingerprint-, and graph-based reaction encodings only partially capture chemical transformations, making accurate prediction difficult for reactions with complex substrates. We propose reaction contrastive learning foundation (RxnCLF), a self-supervised contrastive framework for reaction representation learning. RxnCLF is built on a condensed reaction graph (CRG) that unifies reactant and product information into a single graph, enabling the model to learn explicit and enriched transformation structure rather than disconnected graphs. Pretrained on 1.7 million Pistachio reactions, RxnCLF learns a compact and continuous latent space that captures both reaction-center features and broader side chain contexts, making it transformation-aware and chemically interpretable. Fine-tuned on multiple yield prediction benchmarks, including Buchwald-Hartwig, Pd-catalyzed BH coupling, and proprietary HTE C-N coupling and amide formation datasets, RxnCLF consistently outperforms graph and sequence-based baselines, improving R2 and achieving the best performance overall. Our results highlight the promise of CRG-based RxnCLF as a scalable reaction foundation model, with the potential to generalize across broader reaction spaces and support diverse downstream reaction informatics tasks, including regioselectivity prediction, enantioselectivity prediction, and reaction condition optimization.

</details>


### [278] [Hypothesis Testing with Conditional Queries: Learnability and the Value of Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06262)
*Zonghuan Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究模型评估中自适应测试（根据早期响应选择后续测试）与非自适应测试（预先固定所有测试）之间的查询复杂度差异。在有限结果空间上，作者证明了两类分布可区分当且仅当其成对条件概率存在正分离；当分离为零时，最坏情形错误率恰为1/2。作者构建了一个随机非自适应过程，仅需O(N²(T+log(1/ρ)))对查询即可在总变差距离ρ内模拟任何T查询自适应策略的转录，并证明最坏情形下固定误差的自适应差距为Θ_ε(N²)，说明交互能将所需测试数降低二次因子，但不会产生指数级查询优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型评估中，自适应测试（根据先前响应动态选择后续测试）与非自适应测试（预先固定所有测试）在查询效率上的本质差异尚不清晰。作者希望从理论上明确：何时两类分布能够可靠区分，以及自适应测试相比非自适应测试能带来多大的查询优势。

Method: 论文在条件查询模型下进行研究：(1) 刻画可区分性条件——正分离性判据；(2) 构造随机化非自适应过程，使用O(N²(T+log(1/ρ)))对预先选定的查询，以总变差距离ρ逼近任何T查询自适应策略的转录；(3) 构造匹配下界族，证明常数自适应查询复杂度对应Ω_ε(N²)非自适应查询复杂度。

Result: 研究表明：可学习性当且仅当两分布类的成对条件概率有正分离；分离为零时任意有限查询预算下最优最坏误差恰为1/2。自适应与非自适应的最坏情形固定误差查询差距为Θ_ε(N²)，即交互可带来二次因子的查询复杂度降低。

Conclusion: 交互式评估能减少所需测试数至多为二次因子，但不会产生指数级查询优势。尽管交互过程看似具有指数级分支结构，其在条件查询模型中的实际收益被严格限制在多项式范围内。

Abstract: Model evaluations may fix all tests before observing any responses or select later tests using earlier responses. We study this choice in a conditional-query model on a finite outcome space $\mathcal{X}$ with $|\mathcal{X}|=N$. We first ask which pairs of distribution classes can be reliably distinguished. We then ask how many additional queries are required to match an adaptive tester when all queried events must be fixed in advance. We show that learnability holds if and only if the two classes have positive separation in their pairwise conditional probabilities. When this separation is zero, the optimal worst-case error is exactly $1/2$ at every finite query budget. For any $T$-query adaptive policy and any $ρ\in (0,1)$, we construct a randomized non-adaptive procedure using $O(N^2(T + \log(1/ρ)))$ pair queries chosen before any response is observed. Its simulated transcript is within $ρ$ in total variation of the adaptive transcript, uniformly over all distributions in the model. We also construct a matching family with constant adaptive query complexity and $Ω_\varepsilon(N^2)$ non-adaptive query complexity. Consequently, the worst-case fixed-error adaptivity gap is $Θ_\varepsilon(N^2)$. Thus interaction can reduce the required number of tests by a quadratic factor, but the apparent exponential branching of an interactive evaluation does not yield an exponential query advantage.

</details>


### [279] [The Tamed Subgradient Unadjusted Langevin Algorithm beyond Convexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06283)
*Iosif Lytras,Nikolaos Makras,Sotirios Sabanis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SG-TULA算法，一种直接基于次梯度操作的非光滑Langevin采样算法，通过taming技术处理超线性梯度增长和非凸势能，在Wasserstein-2距离下给出显式常数追踪的非渐近收敛界，并在GPT-2系列LLM的正则化预训练势能上验证了假设，其坐标增强变体在预训练任务上与微调的AdamW和Muon竞争。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有次梯度型Langevin算法在处理同时满足非光滑、超线性梯度增长和非凸三种特性的目标分布时缺乏高效方法。光滑化过程计算代价高，而直接操作次梯度虽有前景，但收敛率尚不理想，且缺乏实际应用验证。

Method: 提出Subgradient Tamed Unadjusted Langevin Algorithm (SG-TULA)：直接对势能的次梯度进行操作以规避光滑化步骤；采用taming技术处理超线性梯度增长，保证显式格式的稳定性；在Wasserstein-2距离下推导非渐近收敛界，所有常数明确追踪为维度和逆温度的函数；设计了提升的坐标wise变体用于大规模预训练。

Result: 收敛率优于现有次梯度型Langevin算法；为GPT-2序列LLM的正则化预训练势能验证了所有假设并给出显式常数；提升的坐标wise变体SG-TULA在预训练任务上达到与微调后的AdamW和Muon相当的竞争性能。

Conclusion: SG-TULA为同时具备非光滑、超线性梯度增长和非凸性的目标分布提供了第一个兼具严格非渐近保证和高计算效率的采样方案。值得注意的是，该方法是有非渐近理论保证的LLM预训练方案，而AdamW和Muon目前缺乏此类可比较的保证。

Abstract: We study the problem of sampling from target distributions whose potentials are simultaneously non-smooth, subject to superlinear gradient growth, and non-convex. We introduce the Subgradient Tamed Unadjusted Langevin Algorithm (SG-TULA), a discretisation of the Langevin diffusion that operates directly on subgradients, without relying on computationally demanding smoothing procedures. To handle the superlinear regime, taming techniques are employed to produce a stable, explicit scheme. We derive non-asymptotic convergence bounds in Wasserstein-2 distance, with all constants tracked explicitly in terms of dimension and inverse temperature, improving upon the currently known rates for subgradient-based Langevin algorithms. We further provide excess risk estimates for the associated optimisation problem. We verify the assumptions, with explicit constants, for the regularized pretraining potential of a LLM in the GPT-2 lineage and the boosted coordinate-wise variant of SG-TULA pretrains the former competitively against finetuned AdamW and Muon, for which no comparable non-asymptotic guarantees are presently available.

</details>


### [280] [Surv-IPTB: An Attention-Based Model for Estimating Individual Probability of Treatment Benefit with Survival Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06288)
*Lev V. Utkin,Stanislav K. Kogan,Andrei V. Konstantinov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Surv-IPTB，一种基于注意力机制的框架，用于在生存分析中估计个体治疗获益概率（IPTB）。该模型将IPTB估计重新表述为二元分类问题，利用治疗组和对照组之间的成对患者比较，并通过不精确概率表示处理右删失数据，使用可学习查询-键变换的注意力机制实现数据驱动的成对比较聚合。在具有复杂非线性结构的合成数据集上（螺旋形、钟形、圆形特征空间），该模型在不同删失率和治疗效果强度下均保持稳健性能，持续优于配备随机生存森林、Cox比例风险模型和Beran估计器的T-learner与S-learner基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化治疗获益评估是精准医疗的核心需求，但现有的meta-learner方法（如T-learner和S-learner）在复杂的非线性场景中性能显著退化。此外，生存分析中的右删失数据使得治疗效果的个体化估计极具挑战性。因此，需要一种能够直接量化特定患者在治疗与对照组之间生存时间延长概率的框架，同时以原则性的方式处理删失观测。

Method: Surv-IPTB框架的核心创新包括：(1) 将IPTB估计重构成治疗组与对照组之间的成对患者比较的二元分类问题；(2) 通过不精确概率表示（区间值概率）原则性地处理右删失观测，为删失案例同时学习软类概率；(3) 采用带可学习查询-键变换的注意力机制，实现灵活的、数据驱动的成对比较聚合。

Result: 在包含螺旋形、钟形和圆形特征空间的复杂非线性合成数据集上进行了广泛实验。与配备随机生存森林、Cox比例风险模型和Beran估计器的T-learner、S-learner基线相比，该模型在不同删失率和治疗效果强度下表现稳健，尤其在传统方法显著退化的非线性场景中持续优于所有基线。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于注意力的框架被验证为生存环境中个性化治疗获益评估的可扩展且统计上原则性的解决方案，模型代码已公开发布。

Abstract: This work presents a novel attention-based framework for estimating the Individual Probability of Treatment Benefit (IPTB) in survival analysis contexts. The proposed model, called Surv-IPTB, directly quantifies the probability that a specific patient will experience extended survival time under treatment versus control. We reformulate IPTB estimation as a binary classification problem, leveraging pairwise patient comparisons across treatment and control cohorts. The framework incorporates a principled handling of right-censored observations through imprecise probability representations, where uncertain treatment effects are characterized by interval-valued probabilities. An attention mechanism with learnable query-key transformations enables flexible, data-driven aggregation of pairwise comparisons, while simultaneously learning soft class probabilities for censored cases. Through extensive experiments on synthetic datasets with complex nonlinear structures, including spiral, bell-shaped, and circular feature spaces, we demonstrate that our approach maintains robust performance across varying censoring rates and treatment effect strengths. The model consistently outperforms meta-learner baselines (T-learner and S-learner) equipped with random survival forests, Cox proportional hazards, and Beran estimators, particularly in challenging nonlinear scenarios where conventional methods exhibit significant degradation. The results establish the proposed attention-based framework as a scalable and statistically principled solution for personalized treatment benefit assessment in survival settings. The code implementing the model is publicly available.

</details>


### [281] [BaKron: Efficient Quantization with Kronecker-Factored Hessians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06291)
*Johann Birnick,Rayan Saab*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了BaKron，一种高效的神经网络量化求解器，利用Kronecker分解的Hessian近似来提供两侧自适应的取整信息，将总计算量从O(m²n²)降至O(mn(m+n))，同时保持与GPTQ相同的立方级顺序复杂度，并在实践中展示了模块化设计和良好性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPTQ风格的自适应取整通常仅利用输入激活的单侧信息，而两侧的Kronecker分解Hessian近似能够捕捉输出坐标间的相关性，从而提供更丰富的曲率几何信息。然而直接在向量化的权重域中应用GPTQ方法计算代价过高，因此需要一个高效的求解器来利用这些双侧信息。

Method: BaKron建立在BoA和YAQA使用的两侧自适应取整公式之上，结合反对角并行（anti-diagonal parallelism）与递归分治（divide-and-conquer）构造。对于m×n的权重矩阵，BaKron仅需O(m+n)的顺序步骤，并将总工作量从O(m²n²)降低到O(mn(m+n))，匹配GPTQ的立方级扩展性同时利用更丰富的曲率信息。BaKron在基础量化器和Hessian估计器方面均具有模块化的设计。

Result: 提供了实际基准测试，考虑了多种可与BaKron配合使用的Hessian变体，发现了一种高效的Hessian计算技术，并进行了实验评估，验证了BaKron的性能和效率。

Conclusion: BaKron通过结合反对角并行和递归分治策略，在保持GPTQ量级计算复杂度的同时充分利用了Kronecker分解Hessian带来的双侧曲率信息，为神经网络量化中的两侧自适应取整问题提供了一个高效且模块化的解决方案。

Abstract: We accelerate a family of algorithms for neural network quantization whose geometry is informed by any Kronecker-factored approximation of the Hessian. GPTQ-style adaptive rounding typically uses one-sided information derived from input activations. Two-sided Kronecker-factored Hessian approximations can additionally capture correlations across output coordinates, but applying GPTQ directly in the vectorized weight domain is computationally expensive. Building on the two-sided adaptive-rounding formulation used by BoA and YAQA, we introduce BaKron, an efficient solver that combines anti-diagonal parallelism with a recursive divide-and-conquer construction. For an $m\times n$ weight matrix, BaKron uses $O(m+n)$ sequential steps while reducing the total work from $O(m^2n^2)$ to $O(mn(m+n))$. Thus, it matches the cubic scaling of GPTQ while exploiting richer curvature information. Moreover, BaKron is modular with respect to both the base quantizer and the Hessian estimator. We also provide practical benchmarks, consider a range of Hessians that BaKron can be called with, find an efficient technique to compute these Hessians, and evaluate the algorithm experimentally.

</details>


### [282] [On-Policy Self-Distillation without Any Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06296)
*Yijiang Li,Bingyang Wang,Yijun Liang,Yunjie Tian,Di Fu,Nuno Vasconcelos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出U-OPSD（无监督在线策略自蒸馏），仅利用模型自身生成内容通过内部一致性实现真正的"自"蒸馏，无需外部监督信号（如真值、环境反馈或大模型指导）。该方法通过多数投票构造伪解，以最短伪解为教师分布条件化，蒸馏至模型最长错误补全的前缀部分，实现精准自我修正。在AIME24、AIME25、HMMT25、MATH500、AMC23等基准上，U-OPSD一致超越基座模型，且匹配或超越有监督方法（如OPSD、GRPO）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的在线策略自蒸馏方法（OPD/OPSD）虽然展现出对LLM后训练的强潜力，但严重依赖外部监督信号（真值标签、环境反馈或更大模型指导），不符合真正的"自"蒸馏理念。作者旨在消除对外部监督的依赖，仅凭模型自身生成结果实现有效的自蒸馏。

Method: U-OPSD包含两个关键步骤：(1) 采样多条rollout，在自一致性阈值下通过多数投票构造伪解；(2) 将教师分布条件化于最短的伪解，并将其蒸馏至模型最长错误补全的前缀，使模型在"自信地错误"的位置实现精准自我修正。

Result: 在多个基准、基座模型和训练设置下，U-OPSD一致优于基座模型。在Qwen3非思考模式4B和8B规模上，分别较基座模型提升8.5%和10.7%；较OPSD平均提升3.2%和2.3%。思考模式下，4B规模较OPSD提升0.9%，8B规模持平；分别超越GRPO 0.7%和1.1%。

Conclusion: U-OPSD证明了仅凭模型自身生成和内部一致性即可实现有效的在线策略自蒸馏，在无需任何外部监督的情况下达到或超越有监督方法的效果，为LLM后训练提供了一种真正的无监督自蒸馏范式。

Abstract: On-policy (Self-)Distillation (OPD / OPSD) has shown strong potential for post-training large language models (LLMs). However, existing methods still rely heavily on external supervision, including ground-truth signals, environmental feedback, or guidance from larger models, and therefore fall short of genuine "self"-distillation. In this study, we show that on-policy self-distillation can be achieved using only a model's own generations via internal consistency. We propose Unsupervised On-Policy Self-Distillation (U-OPSD). U-OPSD first samples multiple rollouts and constructs a pseudo-solution by majority vote under a self-consistency threshold. It then conditions a teacher distribution on the shortest pseudo-solution and distills it into prefixes of the model's longest incorrect completion, allowing the model to correct itself precisely where it is confidently wrong. Across diverse benchmarks, base models, and training settings, U-OPSD consistently improves over the base models and matches or surpasses supervised methods with ground truth (GT), such as OPSD and GRPO. On AIME24, AIME25, HMMT25, MATH500, and AMC23, U-OPSD improves over the base model by 8.5% and 10.7% on Qwen3 non-thinking mode at the 4B and 8B scales, respectively, and outperforms OPSD by an average of 3.2% and 2.3%. In thinking mode, U-OPSD remains on par with OPSD, outperforming it by 0.9% at 4B and matching it at 8B, while surpassing GRPO by 0.7% and 1.1%, respectively.

</details>


### [283] [RRC: Unlocking Generative Reward Models in LLM Reinforcement Learning via Ranking-Based Reward Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06310)
*Chenglong Wang,Ziming Zhu,Yifu Huo,Bei Li,Qiaozhi He,Yan Ding,Xiaoyang Hao,Yuxin Gao,Tianhua Zhou,Xiaojia Chang,Tongran Liu,Jingbo Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出排名奖励构建（RRC）方法，通过从相对偏好排名中推导奖励信号，弥合生成式奖励模型与现有RL算法标量评分范式之间的不匹配，在开放式聊天和推理基准上显著提升RL训练效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式奖励模型在响应排序上表现出色，但未能在强化学习中发挥其潜力。作者分析发现根本原因在于生成式奖励建模的比较性本质与现有RL算法采用的标量评分范式之间存在不匹配，导致RL算法无法有效利用生成式奖励模型的能力。

Method: 提出排名奖励构建（RRC）方法，包含两种互补策略：(1) 自竞争排名，利用采样响应之间的比较；(2) 锚点引导排名，通过少量参考响应实现可扩展的基于排名的奖励构建。

Result: 在开放式聊天和推理基准上的实验表明，RRC显著改善了生成式奖励模型的RL训练效果，相比现有奖励构建方法取得一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: RRC成功弥合了生成式奖励模型与强化学习范式之间的差距，通过排名机制为RL提供更有效的学习信号，代码已开源。

Abstract: Recent advances in reward modeling show a paradigm shift from discriminative reward models to generative reward models. However, despite their strong capabilities in response ranking, generative reward models have not realized their potential in reinforcement learning (RL). Our analysis reveals that this limitation arises from a mismatch between the comparative nature of generative reward modeling and the scalar scoring paradigm adopted by existing RL algorithms. To bridge this gap, we propose a Ranking-based Reward Construction (RRC) approach, which enables generative reward models to provide more effective RL learning signals by deriving rewards from relative preference rankings. RRC introduces two complementary strategies: self-competitive ranking, which exploits comparisons among sampled responses, and anchor-guided ranking, which enables scalable ranking-based reward construction with a small set of reference responses. Experiments across open-ended chat and reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that RRC substantially improves RL training with generative reward models, achieving consistent gains over existing reward construction approaches. Our code can be found at https://github.com/wangclnlp/RRC.

</details>


### [284] [CalibForge: Adversarial Solver Calibration for Scaling Learnable Terminal Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06352)
*Fanzhe Meng,Guoxin Chen,Jiale Zhao,Shuang Sun,Zhiyu Lin,Wayne Xin Zhao,Ruihua Song,Ji-Rong Wen,Kai Jia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CalibForge，一个自主终端任务合成系统，通过多求解器校准和对比求解器校准两种策略，基于已验证的求解器行为对候选任务进行修订，构造出5,431个校准后的终端任务。实验表明这种方法比单纯的作者验证或单求解器反馈产生更有效的监督信号，训练出的模型在Terminal-Bench 2.0上达到32.58%和47.57%的准确率，相较基础模型最大提升达24.71个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练终端智能体需要可执行且可验证的任务，但现有方法仅验证任务的可执行性，无法揭示任务相对于特定求解器设置的学习难度。需要一种方法来自动合成既可行又具有适当挑战性的任务。

Method: 提出CalibForge系统，包含两种校准策略：(1) 多求解器校准，针对异构求解器池内的不一致性；(2) 对比求解器校准，针对指定的强通过/弱失败关系。两种策略都将求解器相对的可学习区域建立在已验证的可解性基础上，通过求解器的实际行为反馈来修订候选任务。

Result: 构建了5,431个校准终端任务；消融实验表明两种策略都优于仅作者验证和普通单求解器反馈；在Terminal-Bench 2.0上达到32.58%和47.57%的准确率；相较于基础模型的最大提升在Terminal-Bench 2.0上为24.71个百分点、SWE-bench Pro上为27.68个百分点、Doc2Repo上为30.04个百分点。

Conclusion: 求解器相对的可学习性可作为构建有效且可迁移的智能体训练数据的实用目标，该方法的有效性在多个基准上得到验证。

Abstract: Training terminal agents requires executable and verifiable tasks that are not merely solvable, but appropriately challenging for learning. Executable validation establishes feasibility, yet does not reveal how a task behaves relative to a given solver setting. In this paper, we present CalibForge, an autonomous terminal-task synthesis system that uses verified solver behavior to revise candidate tasks through adversarial solver calibration. Multi-solver calibration targets disagreement within a heterogeneous solver pool, whereas contrastive solver calibration targets a designated strong-pass/weak-fail relation; both operationalize a solver-relative learnable zone anchored in demonstrated solvability. Using CalibForge, we construct 5,431 calibrated terminal tasks. Our ablations show that both strategies yield more effective supervision than authoring and validation alone or ordinary single-solver feedback. Models trained on the full collection achieve 32.58% and 47.57% on Terminal-Bench 2.0. The largest improvements over the corresponding base model reach 24.71 percentage points on Terminal-Bench 2.0, 27.68 points on SWE-bench Pro, and 30.04 points on Doc2Repo. Together, these results support solver-relative learnability as a practical target for constructing effective and transferable agent training data.

</details>


### [285] [An Optimal Agnostic PAC Algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06363)
*Markus Engelund Mathiasen,Jian Qian,Nikita Zhivotovskiy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对有限VC维d≥1的假设类H⊆{-1,+1}^X，构造了一种学习器，实现了统计最优的风险界，解决了agnostic PAC学习在固定L*处的样本复杂度问题，常数上匹配Devroye、Györfi和Lugosi的下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在agnostic PAC学习中，二元风险L的有限VC维假设类上的最优风险界长期缺乏精确的常数匹配上界。尽管已有下界表明该形式不可避免，但构造一个同时达到统计最优风险界（含L*依赖项）的学习器一直是一个开放问题。

Method: 构造了一个特定的学习器（learner），基于i.i.d.样本，对任意0<δ≤1/2，以至少1-δ的概率，使得输出假设h的风险上界为L* + O(√(L*(d+log(1/δ))/n) + (d+log(1/δ))/n)，其中常数为7×10^8。该结果通过匹配Devroye-Györfi-Lugosi的下界，证明在固定L*处样本复杂度达到了统计最优。

Result: 成功构造了达到统计最优风险界的学习器，风险界为L*加上O(√(L*(d+log(1/δ))/n) + (d+log(1/δ))/n)，常数为7×10^8，并证明了在固定L*处与已知下界相匹配，从而解决了agnostic PAC学习的样本复杂度问题。

Conclusion: 本文在固定每个L*值下，给出了与下界匹配的构造性上界，从而在通用常数意义上彻底解决了有限VC维假设类上agnostic PAC学习的样本复杂度问题。

Abstract: Let $H\subseteq\{-1,+1\}^X$ be a class of finite VC dimension $d\ge1$. Writing $L$ for the binary risk and $L^*=\min_{h\in H}L(h)$, we construct a learner achieving the statistically optimal risk bound: from an i.i.d.\ sample of size $n$, for every $0<δ\le 1/2$, with probability at least $1-δ$, \[
  L(\widehat h)
  \le L^*+ 7\cdot10^8\left(
  \sqrt{\frac{L^*(d+\log(1/δ))}{n}}
  +\frac{d+\log(1/δ)}{n}
  \right). \] This settles the sample complexity of agnostic PAC learning up to universal constants at every fixed $L^*$, matching the lower bounds of Devroye, Györfi, and Lugosi [A Probabilistic Theory of Pattern Recognition, Springer, 1996].

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [286] [VLAff: Vision-Language-Affordance Model for Unified Actionable Affordances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05215)
*Jihoon Oh,Kento Kawaharazuka,Kei Okada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出VLAff框架，从第一人称人类视频中提取与本体无关的可操作affordances（视觉、抓取、轨迹），并构建大规模数据集EgoAffordance（204K片段、560万视觉affordances、1160万抓取与轨迹affordances）。VLAff是基于大视觉语言模型的统一基础模型，能够根据视觉观察和指令生成视觉affordance热图、抓取姿态和轨迹，并可转换为机器人可执行动作，在视觉affordance预测上达到SOTA，并成功应用于真实机器人零样本操作和affordance引导的机器人学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从人类视频学习操作技能对规模化机器人学习有巨大潜力，但人类与机器人之间的本体（embodiment）差异是核心挑战。现有方法难以将人类演示直接迁移到机器人上，需要一种与本体无关的通用表征来弥合这一差距，因此作者提出学习以物体为中心的、可操作的affordances作为机器人可学习的中间表征。

Method: 1) 利用第一人称人类视频，结合3D Structure-from-Motion和手部网格重建技术，提取三类可操作affordances：视觉affordance（在哪里交互）、抓取affordance（如何抓取）、轨迹affordance（如何移动）。2) 构建EgoAffordance大规模数据集。3) 提出VLAff，基于大视觉语言模型（VLM）的统一基础模型，学习所有可操作affordances之间的跨模态相关性。4) 给定视觉观察和指令，VLAff生成视觉affordance热图、抓取姿态和轨迹，利用3D场景信息转换为直接可执行的动作。

Result: VLAff在视觉affordance预测任务上达到最先进性能，并成功部署到真实机器人场景中，包括零样本操作和affordance引导的机器人学习，展现了良好的跨本体迁移能力和实际应用价值。

Conclusion: 本文验证了从人类第一人称视频提取与本体无关的可操作affordances，并利用大视觉语言模型统一建模这些affordances的可行性。该方法有效解决了人类到机器人的embodiment mismatch问题，为大规模、可泛化的机器人操作学习提供了新路径。

Abstract: Learning manipulation skills from human videos is promising for scalable robot learning. However, the embodiment mismatch between humans and robots makes this challenging. One promising solution is to learn object-centric actionable affordances that are embodiment-agnostic. In this work, we propose a framework that leverages egocentric human videos with state-of-the-art 3D Structure-from-Motion and hand mesh reconstruction to extract actionable affordances such as visual, grasp, and trajectory affordances that explicitly encode where to interact, how to grasp, and how to move. We construct EgoAffordance, a large-scale dataset comprising 204K episodes with 5.6M visual affordances and 11.6M grasp and trajectory affordances. Building on this, we introduce VLAff, a large vision-language model-based unified foundation model that learns cross-modal correlations across all actionable affordances. Given a visual observation and instruction, VLAff generates visual affordance heatmaps, grasp poses, and trajectories, which are then converted into directly executable actions by utilizing 3D scene information. Through extensive experiments, we demonstrate that VLAff not only achieves state-of-the-art performance on visual affordance prediction, but can also be effectively applied to real robot applications such as zero-shot manipulation and affordance-guided robot learning.

</details>


### [287] [A System for Train Condition Monitoring and Structural Health Assessment of Rail Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05221)
*Maximilian Posner,Martin Dazer,Daniela Lauer,Robert Winkler-Höhn,Mathilde Laporte,Tobias Herrmann,Martin Köppel*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将结构传感器技术与基于AI的数据分析相结合的实时车辆状态监测与冲击检测方法，旨在克服主线路铁路在完全自动化运行（GoA4）中面临的安全挑战。该框架涵盖冲击检测、基于状态的维护和长期数据驱动的设计优化三大应用，实验结果表明其可行性和潜在价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铁路系统数字化和AI应用正在深刻改变轨道车辆的设计、运行和维护方式。虽然GoA4级别的完全自动化运行已在地铁系统中成熟应用，但在主线路铁路中部署仍受限，主要原因是严格的安全要求和开放运行环境的复杂性。现有的基于摄像头、雷达和激光雷达的感知系统虽能有效检测物体，但在可靠识别撞击、碰撞和碾压事件方面能力有限，亟需新的解决方案。

Method: 提出了一种集成结构传感器技术与AI数据分析的实时车辆状态监测和冲击检测框架。该方法将结构传感器（如应变传感器、加速度传感器等）部署在车辆结构上，通过AI算法对传感器数据进行实时分析，以自动检测冲击、结构损伤和碾压事件。

Result: 实验结果证明了所提出方法的可行性，展示了其在增强运行安全性、实现预测性维护策略以及支持主线路铁路系统向完全自动化运行过渡方面的潜力。该框架可有效应用于三个关键场景：冲击与损伤自动检测、基于状态的连续监测维护、以及支持车辆设计优化的长期数据分析。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架为克服主线路铁路GoA4自动化的关键障碍提供了可行路径，通过结构感知与AI分析相结合，可实现可靠的冲击检测和车辆状态监测，从而增强运行安全性并支持向完全自动化运营的过渡。

Abstract: The ongoing digitalization of rail systems and the increasing use of artificial intelligence (AI) are fundamentally transforming the design, operation, and maintenance of rail vehicles. While fully automated operation at Grade of Automation 4 (GoA4) is well established in metro systems, its deployment in mainline rail remains limited. This is primarily due to stringent safety requirements and the complexity of open operational environments. Current perception systems based on cameras, radar, and lidar are effective in detecting objects but provide limited capability for reliably identifying impacts, collisions, and driving-over events. This paper presents a novel approach for real-time vehicle condition monitoring and impact detection that integrates structural sensor technologies with AI-based data analysis. The proposed framework addresses three key applications: (1) automated detection of impacts, structural damage, and driving-over events, (2) condition-based maintenance enabled by continuous monitoring, and (3) long-term data analytics to support vehicle design optimization. The results demonstrate the feasibility of the proposed approach and highlight its potential to enhance operational safety, enable predictive maintenance strategies, and support the transition toward fully automated operation in mainline rail systems

</details>


### [288] [Failing Gracefully: Mitigating Impact of Inevitable Robot Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05313)
*Duc M. Nguyen,Saad A. Ghani,Andrew Marshall,Allison Andreyev,Gregory J. Stein,Xuesu Xiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的机器人安全规划框架，评估故障期间机器人与周围实体之间影响性交互的概率及其后果严重程度，并引入FailBench（基于MuJoCo的仿真框架）用于在多种故障模式下系统评估机器人-环境交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 服务机器人在家庭环境中与人类、宠物和日常物体共享空间，极易遭受软件崩溃、硬件退化或不可预测交互等故障。虽然机器人专家努力最小化故障，但部分故障不可避免，因此必须缓解其潜在后果以确保安全可靠的部署。

Method: 提出一种新的安全公式（safety formulation），同时评估：1）故障期间机器人与周围实体之间影响性交互的概率；2）这些交互结果的严重程度。通过量化故障对不同实体的影响，使机器人能够在安全性与任务效率之间做出平衡的规划决策。同时提出FailBench，一个基于MuJoCo的仿真框架，支持在感知问题和执行器故障等多种故障模式下研究机器人-环境交互。

Result: 本文提出的安全公式与FailBench仿真框架共同为在真实家庭环境中开发更安全、更稳健的运动规划和强化学习策略提供了基础。实验结果被认为是通过系统评估框架支撑了所提方法的有效性和实用性。

Conclusion: 安全公式与FailBench的组合为家庭环境中服务机器人的安全部署提供了系统性基础，能够在考虑故障影响的同时平衡安全与任务效率，有助于开发更安全稳健的运动规划和策略学习方法。

Abstract: Service robots operate in household environments shared with humans, pets, and everyday objects, where they are highly susceptible to failures such as software crashes, hardware degradation, or unpredictable interactions. While roboticists strive to minimize failures, some remain inevitable, making it critical to mitigate their potential consequences for safe and reliable deployment. This paper introduces a novel safety formulation that evaluates both the probability of impactful interactions between robots and surrounding entities during failures, and the severity of their outcomes. By quantifying the impact of failures on different entities, our approach enables robots to make informed planning decisions that balance safety with task efficiency. To support systematic evaluation, we also present FailBench, a MuJoCo-based simulation framework for studying robot-environment interactions under diverse failure modes, including sensing issues and actuator malfunctions. Together, our safety formulation and FailBench provide a foundation for developing safer and more robust motion plans and learned policies in real-world household environments.

</details>


### [289] [Unified Planning-Learning Framework for Robust UUV Navigation Under Partial Observability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05365)
*Md Ether Deowan,Eleni Kelasidi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种仅依赖观测的UUV（无人水下航行器）自主导航框架，结合持久占用地图、全局间隙感知规划与基于强化学习的局部风险感知控制，并通过行为树蒸馏与不确定性校准机制提升部分可观测环境下导航的安全性和鲁棒性，在NVIDIA Isaac Sim高保真仿真中验证了其优于纯行为树和标准RL基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态水下环境中的UUV导航面临部分可观测性、传感器噪声和动态障碍物等挑战，现有方法在长期规划与短期反应式避障之间的平衡不足，且缺乏可复现的评估协议。

Method: 框架包含四个核心组件：(1) 基于声呐和深度图像的持久占用地图构建；(2) 间隙约束的全局规划器提供长时域结构；(3) 强化学习策略处理短距离跟踪与反应式避障；(4) 从传感器数据中学习的紧凑潜在状态表征编码环境结构、障碍动态和不确定性。提出带分级监督的行为树蒸馏以提升安全性和训练稳定性，并采用基于在线潜在模型不确定性的重加权蒸馏机制，强调高不确定区域的学习。时间到碰撞（TTC）和间隙线索显式保留在规划和局部策略特征中。

Result: 在高保真GPU加速仿真（NVIDIA Isaac Sim）中采用多种子可复现评估协议，与纯行为树和标准RL基线对比，所提框架在动态条件下展现出更优的鲁棒性和安全性。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个统一的混合规划-学习架构与可复现的方法论，为部分可观测条件下鲁棒的UUV自主导航提供了通用流程。

Abstract: This paper presents an observation-only autonomy framework for Unmanned Underwater Vehicles (UUVs) navigation in dynamic underwater environments that integrates persistent occupancy mapping, global clearance-aware planning, and risk-aware local control. The proposed pipeline constructs occupancy maps solely from onboard sonar and depth image observations, adapts a clearance-constrained global planner (GP) to provide long-horizon structure, and integrates a reinforcement learning (RL) policy to handle short-range tracking and reactive avoidance. To further support decision-making under partial observability, the system learns a compact latent state representation from onboard sensor data, encoding environmental structure, obstacle dynamics, and uncertainty. Behavior tree (BT) distillation with staged supervision is introduced to improve safety and training stability, while an uncertainty-calibrated distillation mechanism reweights teacher guidance using online latent-model uncertainty, emphasizing uncertain regimes during learning, with time-to-collision (TTC) and clearance cues remaining explicit in planning and local policy features. To demonstrate the efficacy of the framework, a reproducible multi-seed evaluation protocol is established in high-fidelity GPU-accelerated simulation using NVIDIA Isaac Sim, and performance is benchmarked against BT-only and standard RL baselines. The results obtained demonstrate improved robustness and safety under dynamic conditions, thus providing a general pipeline with a unified hybrid planning learning architecture and a reproducible methodology for robust UUV autonomy under partial observability.

</details>


### [290] [World-to-Wrist: Task-Conditioned Future Wrist Modeling for Fine-Grained Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05369)
*Yuhao Pan,Haosong Peng,Zhengshen Zhang,Zhengyang Yan,Yalun Dai,Fushuo Huo,Chujie Wang,Tianyu Qi,Xiucheng Wang,Nan Cheng,Wenchao Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: W2-VLA提出了一种面向细粒度机器人操作的世界-手腕视觉-语言-动作模型，通过任务条件下的未来手腕建模来区分主视角和手腕视角的独特作用，并引入W2-CoT合成管线生成结构化注解来辅助训练。在LIBERO、RoboTwin 2.0和真实世界任务中取得了改进，同时保持80Hz以上的动作生成速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型将主视角和手腕视角观察视为并行的视觉输入，忽略了它们在机器人操作中各自不同的角色。然而，细粒度操作受益于在手部全局任务上下文下预测手腕局部交互如何演变的能力。

Method: W2-VLA将一组潜在建模token作为视觉-语言模型与手腕预测器之间的紧凑接口。预测器以该接口和观察到的手腕历史为条件，预测未来的手腕潜在表示，并将其转化为未来感知上下文用于动作预测。同时提出W2-CoT合成管线，生成描述操作进度、物理转换线索和手腕局部证据的结构化注解，作为辅助监督来塑造任务条件化的潜在接口。

Result: 在LIBERO、RoboTwin 2.0和真实世界操作任务上的实验表明，W2-VLA在单臂和双臂场景下都改进了细粒度和接触敏感操作，同时保持了80Hz以上的动作生成频率。

Conclusion: 通过区分主视角与手腕视角的不同作用，并以任务条件化的未来手腕建模为核心，W2-VLA有效提升了细粒度操作能力，验证了未来手腕感知上下文对于精确操作的重要性。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models often treat main-view and wrist-view observations as parallel visual inputs, overlooking their distinct roles in robot manipulation. Fine-grained manipulation, however, benefits from anticipating how wrist-local interactions may evolve under the global task context. To address this limitation, we present World-to-Wrist VLA (W2-VLA), a VLA model for fine-grained robot manipulation with task-conditioned future wrist modeling. Given current multi-view observations and a task instruction, W2-VLA contextualizes a set of latent modeling tokens as a compact interface between the vision-language model and the wrist predictor. Conditioned on this interface and the observed wrist history, the predictor forecasts future wrist latents, which are transformed into future-aware context for action prediction. In addition, we introduce W2-CoT, a synthesis pipeline that produces structured annotations describing manipulation progress, physical transition cues, and wrist-local evidence. These annotations provide auxiliary supervision that shapes the task-conditioned latent interface. Experiments on LIBERO, RoboTwin 2.0, and real-world manipulation tasks demonstrate improved fine-grained and contact-sensitive manipulation across both single-arm and bimanual settings, while maintaining action-generation rates above 80 Hz.

</details>


### [291] [Sliding Sensors: Configurable Confidence in State Estimation for Continuum Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05410)
*Ella Walsh,Spencer Teetaert,Eric Diller,Timothy D. Barfoot,Jessica Burgner-Kahrs*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种机械可重构传感方案，通过在连续体机器人内部实现传感器纵向平移，实现状态估计中不确定性重塑，从而在任务相关位置获得足够的估计置信度，并减少全身形状估计误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续体机器人常在不确定环境中作业，准确的状态估计对安全交互至关重要。然而估计不确定性在空间上本质上是不均匀的，且全局估计精度并非总是最高优先级，更重要的是在任务相关位置获得足够置信度。现有固定传感器无法灵活调整置信度分布，因此需要可重构传感机制来实现不确定性重塑。

Method: 提出机械可重构传感概念，设计了传感器在连续体机器人内部纵向平移的硬件方案。通过随时间滑动传感器位置，动态调整状态估计的置信度分布，并与固定尖端传感器对比验证其效果。

Result: 证明了状态估计置信度可通过改变传感器位置进行重构，并且相较于固定尖端传感器，来回滑动传感器能降低全身形状估计误差。

Conclusion: 机械可重构传感为连续体机器人的状态估计提供了一种有效的不确定性塑形手段，尤其适用于对局部估计精度有更高要求的任务场景。

Abstract: Continuum robots often operate in uncertain environments, where accurate state estimation is essential for safe interactions. Estimate uncertainty is inherently spatially non-uniform: confidence varies depending on where measurements are available. Global estimation accuracy is not always the top priority, but rather achieving sufficient confidence at task-relevant locations along the robot. This extended abstract introduces mechanically reconfigurable sensing enabling uncertainty-shaping in state estimation for continuum robots. We present a concept hardware design demonstrating the feasibility of longitudinal translation of a sensor within a continuum robot. We demonstrate that state estimation confidence can be reconfigured by varying the sensor location, and show a reduction of full-body shape estimation errors when sliding the sensor back and forth over time, compared to a single fixed tip sensor.

</details>


### [292] [ARGUS: Aligning Robot Scene Geometry Under Shifting Views with Large 3D Vision Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05579)
*Rishik Sathua,Haonan Chen,Katherine Driggs-Campbell*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出ARGUS，一个利用大规模3D视觉模型将任意相机视角的图像观测对齐到统一规范视角的预处理管线，显著提升了视点多样化数据下视觉运动策略的学习效率和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型视觉运动策略常将场景几何与相机视角纠缠在一起，导致策略学习的是物体在图像中的位置而非任务空间中的位置，这限制了策略从视点多样化数据集（如DROID、BridgeV2）中学习的能力，也无法泛化到训练数据之外的视角。

Method: ARGUS是一个观测预处理管线，利用大规模3D视觉模型将任意相机视角的图像观测对齐到一个规范（canonical）视角，再传递给下游的视觉运动策略。该方法在不同程度视点多样性的训练数据（从固定多相机配置到高度变化的相机位置）上进行了验证。

Result: 实验表明，ARGUS在有限视角和视角多样化的训练制度下均一致优于先前方法；在效率对比中，ARGUS通过简化观测空间，收敛到高成功率的速率比先前方法快4-6倍。

Conclusion: 利用大规模3D视觉模型可以减少视觉运动策略的学习负担，使其能够更高效地从大规模、视点多样化的机器人数据集中进行学习。

Abstract: Large-scale visuomotor policies have demonstrated impressive performance across a wide range of robot manipulation tasks. However, despite this success, manipulation polices often entangle scene geometry with the corresponding viewpoint, learning where objects lie in an image rather than where it lies in the task space. This entanglement inherently limits the corresponding policy's ability to learn from viewpoint-diverse datasets (ex. DROID, BridgeV2) and generalize beyond the viewpoints captured in their training data. In this work, we present ARGUS, an observation pre-processing pipeline that uses large-scale 3D vision models to align image observations from arbitrary camera viewpoints into a canonical viewpoint before passing it to downstream visuomotor policies. Experiments across training datasets with varying levels of viewpoint diversity, from fixed multi-view camera configurations to highly varied camera placements, show that our method consistently outperforms prior approaches across both limited-view and view-diverse training regimes. In efficiency comparisons, ARGUS demonstrates an ability to learn from view-diverse data, converging to high success rates 4-6x faster than previous methods by leveraging a simplified observation space. Overall, our findings show that leveraging large-scale 3D vision models reduces the learning burden on visuomotor policies, enabling more efficient learning from large-scale, viewpoint-diverse robot datasets.

</details>


### [293] [PathCover: A Fast Convex Decomposition along a Path via Randomized Iterative Space Partitioning (RISP) on Point Clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05586)
*Kunal S. Narkhede,Abhijeet M. Kulkarni,Guoquan Huang,Ioannis Poulakakis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出PathCover框架，基于新型随机算法RISP直接从原始点云数据构建凸多面体走廊，在期望线性时间内生成无碰撞区域，用于自动驾驶机器人（四旋翼和四足机器人）的实时轨迹规划。该算法在温和的概率消除条件下可保证有限步终止并沿参考路径持续推进，相比现有方法实现一个数量级的加速，同时保持相当的走廊体积。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主机器人导航需要快速生成无障碍区域以用于轨迹规划，但现有的走廊生成器难以满足实时、传感器速率级别的计算约束，成为系统瓶颈。为了解决这一效率问题，作者提出PathCover框架，旨在加速凸多面体走廊的生成过程。

Method: 核心方法为RISP（随机算法），直接从原始点云数据构建凸多面体，在期望线性时间（O(n)）内完成，依赖于一个温和的概率消除条件。PathCover生成一系列重叠的无障碍凸多面体，用于约束下游的MPC（模型预测控制）和轨迹优化。作者从数学上证明了算法在有限步内终止，并保证沿任意无障碍参考路径持续推进。

Result: 在合成和真实LiDAR数据集上的大量基准测试表明，PathCover相比最先进方法实现了数量级的加速，同时保持了相当的走廊体积。整个管线通过高保真四旋翼仿真以及四足机器人实时LiDAR感知的物理部署得到了验证。

Conclusion: PathCover通过随机化线性时间算法有效解决了走廊生成的实时计算瓶颈，在不牺牲走廊质量的前提下大幅提升生成速度，并已在仿真与实物平台上得到充分验证，为自主移动机器人在复杂受限环境中的实时导航提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Autonomous robot navigation requires the rapid generation of obstacle-free regions for trajectory planning. However, existing corridor generators struggle to meet real-time, sensor-rate computational constraints. To resolve this bottleneck, we introduce PathCover, a framework driven by RISP; a novel randomized algorithm that constructs convex polytopes directly from raw point cloud data in expected linear time under a mild probabilistic elimination condition. PathCover generates sequences of overlapping, obstacle-free polytopes that safely constrain downstream MPC and trajectory optimization. We mathematically guarantee that the algorithm terminates in finite steps while ensuring continuous progress along any obstacle-free reference path. Extensive benchmarks on synthetic and real-world LiDAR datasets demonstrate an order-of-magnitude speedup over state-of-the-art methods while maintaining comparable corridor volumes. The complete pipeline is validated via high-fidelity quadrotor simulations and physical deployment on a quadrupedal robot navigating constrained environments using live LiDAR perception.

</details>


### [294] [Search-Aided Joint Agent-Environment Reinforcement Learning for Robust Lifelong Multi-Agent Path Finding with Rotations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05588)
*He Jiang,Jingtian Yan,Yulun Zhang,Yimin Tang,Tanishq Duhan,Rishi Veerapaneni,Guillaume Sartoretti,Jiaoyang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种名为SJRL的搜索辅助联合强化学习方法，用于解决包含鲁棒安全约束和原地旋转约束的现实LMAPF问题（LMAPF-R2），在多种高密度地图及混合现实仓储环境中显著优于强基线Causal-PIBT。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习型LMAPF规划器大多基于过度简化的运动学假设，忽略了真实世界中的关键运动约束（如安全距离和原地旋转），导致其在实际自动化仓储系统中的性能受限。

Method: 提出Search-Aided Joint Reinforcement Learning (SJRL)框架：(1) 将神经策略与Causal PIBT单步搜索规划器结合，用于解决碰撞并传播智能体意图；(2) 引入统一的强化学习公式，联合优化智能体策略与环境策略，其中环境策略学习图边权重，通过反向Dijkstra搜索提供全局移动引导。

Result: 在多个高密度地图上，SJRL相较于强搜索基线Causal-PIBT取得显著改进；并在包含8个实体机器人及248个虚拟机器人的混合现实仓储环境中验证了其有效性与实用性。

Conclusion: 引入运动约束的LMAPF-R2模型更贴近真实仓储场景，SJRL通过神经策略与搜索方法的结合及联合RL优化，能够有效应对高密度、强约束场景下的协调难题，为实际部署提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Lifelong Multi-Agent Path Finding (LMAPF) requires repeatedly planning collision-free paths for agents that continuously receive new goals upon reaching their current ones. While many learning-based planners have been proposed for LMAPF, most rely on oversimplified kinematic assumptions that may overlook motion constraints critical to real-world performance. In this work, we study a more realistic LMAPF model derived from many real-world automated warehouse systems, termed LMAPF-R2, which incorporates robust safety constraints and in-place rotation constraints. These constraints substantially increase coordination difficulty, particularly in highly constrained spaces. To address these challenges, we propose Search-Aided Joint Reinforcement Learning (SJRL). We first augment neural policies with Causal PIBT, a single-step search-based planner that resolves agents' collisions and propagates their intentions. We then introduce a unified RL formulation that jointly optimizes agent and environment policies, where the environment policy learns graph edge costs to provide global movement guidance via backward Dijkstra search. Experiments demonstrate that SJRL achieves significant improvements over the strong search-based planner, Causal-PIBT, across multiple high-density maps. We further validate SJRL in a challenging mixed-reality warehouse environment with 8 physical robots and 248 virtual robots.

</details>


### [295] [KILVO: Kinematic-Inertial-LiDAR-Visual Odometry with Robust Multimodal Adaptation for Humanoid Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05647)
*Jixin Gao,Fucheng Liu,Teng Zhang,Fusheng Zha*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向人形机器人的运动学-惯性-激光雷达-视觉里程计系统KILVO，在异步-顺序混合误差状态迭代卡尔曼滤波器（ESIKF）框架下融合关节编码器、IMU、LiDAR和相机多模态传感器数据，通过高频率腿部运动学约束和顺序更新的外感受信息，实现对传感器故障的鲁棒性并提供高精度、高效率的姿态估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人面临复杂的真实世界环境，需要高精度、高频率且稳健的状态估计。现有融合方法未能充分利用人形机器人上特有的传感器组合（如关节编码器提供的腿部运动学信息），也缺乏针对传感器退化或故障的鲁棒性设计。

Method: 采用异步-顺序混合误差状态迭代卡尔曼滤波器（ESIKF），IMU数据用于状态预测，关节编码器提供的腿部运动学以高频异步处理提供本体感受约束，外感受信息按顺序更新：先通过LiDAR点云配准建立几何先验，再通过光度误差更新视觉分量。框架具有多模态自适应能力以应对传感器故障，并集成了无需额外传感器的紧凑接触估计模块。

Result: 在公开数据集和多台人形机器人、多种步态模式及场景下的真实世界实验中，KILVO在精度、效率、输出频率方面展现出高度竞争力，并对传感器退化和故障具有较强鲁棒性，优于现有最先进的融合方法。

Conclusion: KILVO充分利用人形机器人上常见的多模态传感器配置，通过异步-顺序混合ESIKF框架实现了高精度、高效率且抗传感器故障的姿态估计，比现有最先进融合方法更适合人形机器人应用，代码和数据集已开源。

Abstract: This article presents a kinematic-inertial-LiDAR-visual odometry for humanoid robots, called KILVO. Tailored to the platform features, requirements, and real-world complexity, it fully utilizes the sensors commonly equipped on humanoid robots, including joint encoders, IMU, LiDAR, and camera, within an asynchronous-sequential hybrid error-state iterated Kalman filter (ESIKF). Specifically, inertial data are used for prediction, leg kinematics are processed asynchronously at a high rate and provide proprioceptive constraints, while exteroception is updated sequentially, first by registering LiDAR points for geometric priors and then by updating the visual component via photometric errors. Moreover, the framework is elaborately designed with multimodal adaptation for resilience to sensor failures. A compact contact estimation module is also developed, sharing information with state estimation without additional sensors. Extensive experiments on public datasets and in the real world across multiple humanoid robots, gait patterns, and scenarios demonstrate that KILVO achieves highly competitive accuracy, efficiency, and output rates, with strong robustness against sensor degradation and failures, making it more suitable for humanoid robots than state-of-the-art fusion methods. Our code and datasets are released on GitHub.

</details>


### [296] [JoyAI-RA 0.5: Scaling Robot Manipulation Learning via Dual Action Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05674)
*JoyAI-RA Team*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: JoyAI-RA 0.5是一个通用的视觉-语言-世界-动作(VLWA)框架,通过隐式和显式双重动作对齐,将人类第一视角视频、仿真数据和真实机器人数据统一用于操作技能学习。在真实Agibot基准上,随着人类视频预训练数据量增加,任务得分持续提升且未出现平台期,验证了弱标注人类经验可作为操作能力扩展的主要信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人数据稀缺,通用策略需要从异构数据源(人类第一视角视频、仿真、真实机器人)学习,但这些数据在监督信号和具身形态上存在差异,动作标签缺失或互不兼容。人类第一视角数据规模最大但距机器人数据最远,朴素的数据混合会导致负迁移而非知识共享,因此需要一种能跨数据源对齐并共享知识的方法。

Method: 提出JoyAI-RA 0.5框架,包含三部分:(1) 隐式动作对齐:从视觉转换中推断潜在动作,使无动作标签的人类、仿真和机器人数据能引导潜在动作条件的世界模型学习物理动力学;(2) 显式动作对齐:通过规范化动作表示和相机帧块相对末端执行器动作,将可靠的人类与机器人轨迹统一到物理动作空间;(3) 内外环强化阶段:内环高效任务自适应,外环提升基础策略性能。

Result: 在真实AgiBot基准上,JoyAI-RA在已见任务和未见变体上均表现强劲。任务得分随人类第一视角预训练数据量增加而持续一致提升,在最大规模下仍未见平台期迹象。

Conclusion: 丰富但弱标注的人类经验可以被转化为可迁移的训练信号,使人类视频不仅作为弱辅助数据源,而是成为操作能力扩展的主要维度。该工作验证了跨异构数据源(人类视频、仿真、机器人)协同学习操作技能的可行性。

Abstract: Robot data is scarce, so generalist policies need to learn from heterogeneous sources, including human egocentric video, simulation, and real robots, which differ in supervision and embodiment, with action labels missing or mutually incompatible. Human egocentric data scale best but sit farthest from robot data, and naive pooling causes negative transfer rather than knowledge sharing. We propose JoyAI-RA 0.5, a generalist Vision-Language-World-Action (VLWA) framework that couples physical world-dynamics priors with visual semantics and scales manipulation learning across such data via dual action alignment. Implicit action alignment infers latent actions from visual transitions, enabling action-free human, simulation, and robot data to guide a latent-action-conditioned world model in learning physical dynamics. Explicit alignment grounds reliable human and robot trajectories in a unified physical action space through a canonical action representation and camera-frame chunk-relative end-effector actions. An inner-outer-loop reinforcement stage then pairs efficient task adaptation with foundation-policy improvement. On a real-world AgiBot benchmark, JoyAI-RA performs strongly on both seen tasks and unseen variations. The task score improves consistently as the volume of human egocentric pretraining data increases and shows no sign of plateauing at our largest scale. This suggests that abundant but weakly labeled human experience can be converted into a transferable training signal, making human video not merely a weak auxiliary source but a primary axis along which manipulation capability can be scaled. Project page can be found at https://joyai-ra-05.github.io/.

</details>


### [297] [Nonvisual Classification of Ground-Condition by Artificial Proprioception in an Amoeba-Inspired Autonomous Walking Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05684)
*Hyoto Yamaguchi,Zenji Yatabe,Seiya Kasai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究为仿变形虫自主行走四足机器人提出了一种基于多模态传感的非视觉地面条件分类方法，通过集成三轴加速度计、八个足部压力传感器和储层计算（RC）技术实现人工本体感觉，在行走动态运动导致传感器输出大幅波动的情况下仍能高精度区分平坦与崎岖地面，并在机器人上演示了根据地面条件在线切换行走步态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统地面条件分类依赖视觉传感与图像处理，而如何在缺乏视觉的情况下，让自主行走机器人仅通过自身传感信息可靠地感知地面条件，是一个尚未充分解决的问题。本文旨在探索无需图像传感的非视觉分类方案。

Method: 采用多模态传感方法：集成三轴加速度计与八个足部压力传感器构建人工本体感觉系统，并结合储层计算（RC）算法对传感器时序数据进行处理和学习，以分类地面条件（平坦或粗糙）。

Result: 即使四足机器人在行走过程中产生大幅传感器输出波动，系统仍能以高精度分类地面条件（平坦或粗糙）；成功实现了机器人根据地面条件在线切换行走步态；并分析了各传感器对地面分类的贡献程度。

Conclusion: 基于多模态传感与储层计算的非视觉地面分类方法在仿生行走机器人上有效可行，为无视觉条件下机器人环境感知与自适应步态控制提供了一种新的技术路径，同时揭示了不同传感器模态在分类任务中的差异化贡献。

Abstract: Nonvisual classification of ground condition based on a multimodal sensing approach was investigated for an amoeba-inspired autonomous walking robot. To classify ground condition without image sensing and processing, we implemented artificial proprioception by integrating a three-axis accelerometer, eight foot pressure sensors, and reservoir computing (RC). Even when large fluctuations in the sensor outputs are caused by dynamic motions of a four-legged robot in walking, our system can classify the ground condition, flat or rough, with high accuracy. We demonstrate on-site switching of walking gait depending on ground condition in the robot. We also discuss the contribution of each sensor to ground condition classification.

</details>


### [298] [Hijacking Robots with a Piece of Paper: A Systematic Study of Physical Prompt Injection in VLM-Controlled Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05715)
*S. M . Bhagya P. Samarakoon,M. A. Viraj J. Muthugala,W. K. R. Sachinthana,Mohan Rajesh Elara*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了针对VLM控制机器人分拣系统的物理提示注入攻击，提出了四分类攻击框架，构建了包含20个攻击提示、3种物理场景和3种指令形式的基准测试。在5,670次试验中，攻击在三个前沿VLM（GPT-4o、Gemini 2.5 Flash、Qwen3-VL-32B）上的成功率分别为27.0%、29.4%和5.0%，并评估了三种简单防御方法（提示防御75-100%、两阶段验证85-100%、文本掩蔽100%）的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着VLM越来越多地被部署为机器人系统中的规划器，其在视觉感知与指令遵循之间的紧密耦合引入了新的攻击面：放置在机器人视野中的对抗性文本可作为间接提示注入进入VLM的推理栈。作者旨在系统性地研究这一物理世界的安全威胁，理解其攻击机制、跨模型迁移性及防御策略。

Method: 作者提出包含四类别的攻击分类法：间接标志（indirect signage）、任务重定义（task redefinition）、权威冒充（authority impersonation）和冲突注入（conflict injection）。构建了20个攻击提示的基准测试，横跨3种物理场景布局和3种指令形式（在目的地特定性和规则明确性上变化）。对三个VLM（GPT-4o、Gemini 2.5 Flash、Qwen3-VL-32B）进行5,670次试验，并通过推理轨迹分析模型防御机制，评估三种简化防御策略。

Result: 攻击成功率在GPT-4o、Gemini 2.5 Flash和Qwen3-VL-32B上分别为27.0%、29.4%和5.0%。权威冒充和否定攻击在所有三个模型间可迁移。推理轨迹显示99.9%的成功攻击是"有意识"的（模型明确承认了对抗性指令），不同模型采用结构上不同的防御机制：Gemini采用明确拒绝，GPT-4o采用感知性忽视。三种防御策略有效性为：提示防御75-100%（模型相关）、两阶段验证85-100%、文本掩蔽100%。

Conclusion: VLM控制的操纵系统确实容易受到人类可读物理标志的攻击，但简单防御策略能显著降低风险。防御选择涉及权衡：虽然防御在基准测试中保持了通用任务能力，但可能损害需要读取场景内标签的任务。这提示物理世界中的VLM部署需要针对实际使用场景审慎选择防御策略。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) are increasingly deployed as planners in robotic systems, where they translate natural-language commands into executable actions grounded in visual scene understanding. This tight coupling between perception and instruction-following introduces a new attack surface: adversarial text placed within the robot's visual field can act as an indirect prompt injection into the VLM's reasoning stack. We present a systematic study of physical prompt injection attacks against VLM-controlled sorting, introducing a four-category taxonomy, indirect signage, task redefinition, authority impersonation, and conflict injection, instantiated as a benchmark of 20 attack prompts evaluated across three physical scene layouts and three command formulations that vary in destination specificity and rule explicitness. Across 5,670 trials on three frontier VLMs (GPT-4o, Gemini 2.5 Flash, Qwen3-VL-32B), attacks succeed at 27.0%, 29.4%, and 5.0% respectively, with authority-impersonating and negation attacks transferring across all three models. Analysis of reasoning traces reveals that successful compromise is nearly always conscious (99.9% acknowledgment rate), and that models defend through structurally different mechanisms, explicit rejection for Gemini, perceptual inattention for GPT-4o. We evaluate three simple mitigations: prompt-based defense (75-100% effective, model-dependent), two-stage verification (85-100%), and pre-processing text masking (100%). Our findings show that VLM-controlled manipulation is meaningfully vulnerable to human-readable physical signage, and that simple defenses substantially reduce risk, though defense choice involves trade-offs.
